# WBS7 — Frontend page/component refactor ledger

Status: baseline ledger only; no production source was changed.

Baseline checkpoint: `f6efa8a2f45383005851475c04fc213835b16c4a` (`f6efa8a2`).
Worktree: `refactor/wbs7-frontend-ledger`.

## Scope and reproducible inventory

This ledger is the WBS7 input for the remaining frontend refactor. It records
ownership and extraction targets; it does not claim that the listed moves are
already complete. The inventory deliberately treats a direct `useQuery`,
`useMutation`, transport/client call, or feature API import in a page/component
as an explicit review row. API imports that are only types or boundary props
remain marked for review rather than being called a defect.

Commands used from repository root:

```bash
git rev-parse HEAD
rg --files frontend/src | wc -l
rg --files frontend/src | awk '/\.tsx$/ {n++} END {print n}'
rg --files frontend/src | awk '/\/pages\/.*\.tsx$/ {n++} END {print n}'
rg --files frontend/src | awk '/\/components\/.*\.tsx$/ {n++} END {print n}'
python3 - <<'PY'
import re
from pathlib import Path
s=Path('frontend/src/app/routes/AppRoutes.tsx').read_text()
print('routes=', len(re.findall(r'<Route\s+path="', s)))
PY
python3 - <<'PY'
import re
from pathlib import Path
patterns = (
    re.compile(r'\buseQuery\b|\buseAsyncQuery\b'),
    re.compile(r'\buseMutation\b'),
    re.compile(r'backendTransport|backendClient|\bfetch\s*\('),
    re.compile(r"""from ['"][^'"]*(?:/api|Api|api)['"]|import ['"][^'"]*(?:/api|Api|api)['"]"""),
)
files = []
for path in Path('frontend/src').rglob('*.tsx'):
    text = path.read_text(errors='ignore')
    if any(pattern.search(text) for pattern in patterns):
        files.append(path)
print('direct-marker-tsx=', len(files))
for path in sorted(files):
    print(path)
PY
```

The strict WBS7 work queue is 105 rows (81 query/mutation rows plus 24 direct
API/command rows). The 122-file table below is the broader lexical diagnostic:
it also keeps type-only/boundary API imports visible so they cannot disappear
from review. `Q` = `useQuery`/`useAsyncQuery`, `M` = `useMutation`,
`T` = direct transport/client/fetch, `A` = feature API import.

## Classification and target contract

| Classification | Meaning | Required end state |
|---|---|---|
| `MOVE-QUERY-TO-FEATURE-HOOK` | Page owns query/mutation orchestration | Page calls a feature hook; hook owns query state and invalidation; API owns DTO/mapping |
| `MOVE-QUERY-TO-HOOK-OR-KEEP-LOCAL` | Component owns a use-case query/command | Extract when it represents a reusable use case; otherwise keep a thin component hook with a documented feature contract |
| `MOVE-API-TO-HOOK` | Page/component calls transport/client directly | Move HTTP and mapping to feature `api`; expose a feature hook/command to UI |
| `KEEP-BOUNDARY-REVIEW` | API import may be a valid command boundary, type, or existing feature adapter | Verify no DTO mapping, HTTP, or business rule is in UI; record as no-change if already thin |
| `NO-CHANGE` | Route composition, presentation-only UI, or existing hook/API boundary | Keep; add no new cross-feature/deep import |

Every extracted use case must preserve route, role gate, request/response
contract, error state, idempotency/version behavior, and current test command.
The target dependency remains `page -> component/hook -> feature api/domain/data
-> shared transport`; `app` only composes routes/providers/layout/session.

## Required route rows and no-change rows

`AppRoutes.tsx` has 74 route rows at this checkpoint. The route-facing rows
below are required even when no extraction is planned: `/terms`, `/data-policy`,
`/`, `/owner-registration`, `/chon-trai`, `/dang-ky-trai`,
`/invitation/accept`, and `*` are composition/public/fallback rows. They must
remain route-only and must not gain API/business logic in `app/routes`.

The other route rows map to the feature use cases above, including all WBS4
(`herd-entry`), WBS5 (`herd-exit`), and WBS6 (`thuc-an`) paths. Route guards
(`desk`, `owner`, `ownerFarm`, `farmMember`, `technician`, `cs`, `pm`, `sa`)
are no-change app composition. A route row is complete only when its lazy
loader, role/farm guard, path and page contract remain unchanged.

No-change file rows are generated as the complement of the 122-file direct-I/O
inventory: `rg --files frontend/src --glob '*.tsx'` minus the output of the
direct-marker command above. This is intentional: presentation-only components
and thin route wrappers are not forced into hooks. Re-run the complement after
each slice and record only a changed classification or a new direct marker.

## Explicit blockers and sequencing

1. **Reproduction FE-11/FE-12:** `docs/agent-context/FE-11-12-progress.md`
   records backend 6a complete but frontend 6b (`reproductionBackend.ts`,
   Q5 rules/CalvingPage/demo boundary and adapter tests) and runtime 6c still
   requiring evidence. Do not call this ledger a completed reproduction
   refactor until the adapter contract and the nine QA flows are re-run.
2. **FARM_BORN parentage:**
   `docs/analysis/herd-entry-farm-born-contract-blocker.md` records the
   SRS-read-only versus backend parentage-correction discrepancy. WBS7 must not
   silently resolve it while moving UI code; keep the current command and carry
   the blocker to the product/backend decision.
3. **WBS4–WBS6 preservation:** these are already composed vertical slices. The
   next WBS7 agent must not rewrite their behavior or replace backend adapters
   with mock/local data. A row marked `KEEP-BOUNDARY-REVIEW` is only closed by
   showing a thin feature boundary and the focused test command.

Safe order for parallel work is one isolated feature/worktree per slice:

```text
app/session + auth  → workspace/cattle/pen → care features
       ↘ platform-admin/finance/service/support ↗
```

Do not edit `app/routes/AppRoutes.tsx` concurrently with another route owner;
route composition is the shared-file step and must be sequenced. After each
slice: inspect imports, run its feature-local command, record the changed rows,
and commit. Integration runs `npm run test:api`, integration Vitest,
`npm run typecheck`, and `npm run build` only after approved slices are merged.

## Ledger completion criteria

- Every page/component with a direct marker is represented above exactly once.
- Every route remains represented by an existing lazy loader/guard or an
  explicit public/fallback no-change row.
- Every use-case has a feature hook/API/domain target and a focused test command;
  no page owns DTO mapping or direct transport after its slice is complete.
- No production mock/demo/local fallback is introduced.
- WBS4/WBS5/WBS6 contracts are preserved; reproduction and FARM_BORN blockers
  remain explicit rather than being hidden by a structural move.

| `/nen-tang/ho-so` | `only(['CS', 'PM', 'SA'], <PlatformProfile />)` | only | preserve path/guard; page row above |
| `*` | `<Placeholder />` | public/composition | preserve path/guard; page row above |

## Appendix D — deterministic atomic extraction manifest

Appendix C is the file-level inventory and is intentionally closed: it has
122 source rows, all `Work queue=true`, and zero exclusions. It is not itself
an atomic work queue. Atomic rows must be regenerated from the checked-out
source at dispatch time so that a method is never invented from a prose
summary and a multi-method file is never silently treated as one task.

Use this read-only command from the repository root to emit the atomic rows:

```bash
python3 - <<'PY'
import re
from pathlib import Path

ledger = Path('docs/analysis/frontend-page-refactor-ledger.md').read_text()
section = ledger[ledger.index('## Appendix C'):]
paths = [line.split('|', 2)[1].strip().strip('`')
         for line in section.splitlines()
         if line.startswith('| `frontend/src/')]
assert len(paths) == 122, len(paths)
for path_text in paths:
    path = Path(path_text)
    source = path.read_text()
    operations = []
    for match in re.finditer(r'use(?:Async)?Query\s*\(\s*\(.*?\)\s*=>\s*([^\n]+)', source):
        operations.append(('query', match.group(1).strip()))
    for match in re.finditer(r'useMutation\s*\(', source):
        operations.append(('mutation', 'useMutation'))
    for match in re.finditer(r'\b(?:[A-Za-z_$][\w$]*Api|api)\.([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)', source):
        operations.append(('api', match.group(0)))
    if re.search(r'<(?:Route|Routes)\b|\blazy\s*\(', source):
        operations.append(('page-rewire', 'route composition'))
    if not operations:
        operations.append(('boundary-review', 'type/import boundary'))
    for ordinal, (kind, symbol) in enumerate(operations, 1):
        print(f'{path_text}\t{ordinal}\t{kind}\t{symbol}')
PY
```

The output is the dispatch manifest. Repeated `api.method` entries are
deduplicated by `(source, kind, symbol)` while preserving first source order;
the source file, line number, and symbol are retained in the handoff. A target
hook/export is recorded only after the coding agent verifies that export in
the worktree. Until then it is `UNASSIGNED`, never a guessed file name. Test
paths that do not exist at dispatch time are recorded as `NEW: <path>` rather
than presented as existing coverage.

This makes the two previously ambiguous examples explicit without fabricating
targets: `PaymentReconciliationRoutes.tsx` emits the five actual calls
`listEvidence`, `paymentTiles`, `listPayments`, `getEvidence`, and
`listCandidates`, plus its route/page-rewire operation; the technician
announcement route emits the actual `list`, `get`, and `markRead` calls plus
its route/page-rewire operation.
